# 序

习近平主席在致2024世界中文大会的贺信中指出，语言是人类交流思想的工具、传承文明的载体、增进理解的桥梁。中文承载着中华民族数千年的文明智慧，是中国贡献给世界的重要公共文化产品，支持服务国际社会开展好中文教育是中国作为母语国的责任。

作为中国教育部推出的服务全球中文学习者的标准化语言测量工具，中文水平考试HSK历经三十余年发展，已成为全球最具影响力和权威性的中文水平测评体系。截至目前，HSK已在全球168个国家设立1477个考点、3万余间考场，累计服务考生820万人。HSK不仅成为评估国际中文教育质量的重要标尺，更在吸引国际学生来华留学、助力海外人才在中资及跨国企业就业实习等方面发挥着日益重要的作用。

当前，新一轮科技革命与产业变革加速演进，人工智能等新技术正在重塑教育形态，对人才需求产生深远影响。面对海外中文学习需求的爆发式增长，国际中文教育迎来新的发展机遇，也对HSK的服务能力与测评体系提出更高要求。为此，我们积极推进HSK向3.0版本升级，将原有一至六级考试体系调整为覆盖初、中、高级各水平人群的三等九级新体系，并根据社会经济生活发展，调整HSK话题和词汇，增加中国文化、中国当代发展等考查内容，帮助全球学习者了解真实、立体、全面的中国；加强“中文+学术”“中文+专业”“中文+职业”等内容，帮助国际人才精准掌握中文、拓宽专业领域；推动人工智能全面赋能测评体系，加强AI命题、AI评分及AI自适应学习产品开发，主动适应并引领智能时代的教育变革。

以HSK3.0为依托，汉考国际（CTI）联合外语教学与研究出版社，汇聚专家智慧，开发了《新HSK教程》。本教程坚持以学习者为中心，支持课堂教学、自主学习和AI助学等多种方式，旨在全面提升学习者的中文交际能力、认知水平与思辨素养。很高兴能向各国中文学习者推荐《新HSK教程》，期待这套教程能够助力更多国际学子掌握中文、走向世界，为推动构建人类命运共同体贡献语言之力！

最后，谨向所有参与HSK发展与教程编写的专家同仁、全球合作伙伴致以诚挚谢意！

郁云峰

中国教育部中外语言交流合作中心主任

2025年12月

# 前言

作为新一代多功能教材，《新HSK教程》系列（以下简称“新教程”）的设计、编写继续秉持HSK“以学习者为中心”的理念，并综合借鉴当代语言学、第二语言教学等理论成果，进一步拓展实践空间。“以学习者为中心”的理念强调新教程不是单一的HSK考试用书，而是将HSK考试与中文教学、学习有机融合，通过课堂教学、自主学习、AI助学的方式进一步提升学习者的中文交际能力、认知水平与思辨素养，帮助其成为精通中文、融通中外的优秀人才。

新教程1～6级分为主干课本和学练手册两种，主干课本聚焦课堂教学与核心内容传授，学练手册则是对主干课本内容的延伸和补充。学练手册练习题深度对标HSK3.0考试，学习者在练习的过程中既能了解自身中文掌握情况，也能熟悉HSK3.0考试题型，从而实现从容备考。此外，新教程的主干课本在编写中突出交际情景化、结构简约化，同时借助数字手段使教材资源呈现立体化特征。

由于中文对交际语境高度依赖，情景的重要性不言而喻。为此，新教程借鉴情景剧形式设计交际场景及交际任务，以一系列独立的情景剧单元组织内容。例如，新教程设计了海外和中国国内两类场景，故事线索围绕数位主要角色展开，不仅演绎中文学习者的校园生活、个人成长点滴，也展现中国人的日常生活、学习工作图景，讲述中外交往、友谊生辉的故事，让学习者在真实的场景和故事中学习中文，了解真实、立体、全面的中国。

为满足AI时代的泛在化学习需求，新教程采取教案化、学案化设计流程，更方便教学或自学。新教程编写期间，恰逢AI技术蓬勃发展，数字技术的全方位赋能使得教材资源立体化建设成为可能。新教程设计了一位AI助教——小语，她是将纸版教材和数字资源连接起来的灵魂性人物，学习者找到小语，即可获得相关资源。例如，学习者可以通过“小语助力”“小语讲堂”等板块进行语言知识及语言技能的学习，通过敲“小语的彩蛋”获取更多数字资源，既有趣又方便。未来我们还将持续开发线上课程，让小语成为您教中文、学中文的好助手。

新教程策划及编写过程中，得到了中外语言交流合作中心、汉考国际、外语教学与研究出版社以及业内许多专家学者的鼎力支持与专业指导。现在，这套凝聚着集体智慧的教材终于和大家见面了，我们诚挚欢迎海内外使用者及时反馈使用中遇到的问题，帮助新教程不断完善。感谢您的支持！

编者

2025年12月

# 使用说明

《新HSK教程1》适用于零起点中文学习者及预备参加HSK（一级）考试的学习者。

##### 基本设计

依据HSK新大纲和本系列教程设计理念、原则，《新HSK教程1》全书共设15课，词汇匹配新大纲规定的300词，略有扩展，语言点40个。总授课时数建议30~36学时，每课建议授课时间为2~3学时。

《新HSK教程1》秉持“以学习者为中心”理念，坚持目标导向、需求导向，以培养学习者中文交际能力为原则，将系统的拼音知识和汉字知识学习分别置于第1课和第7课“小语的彩蛋”中，同时置于《新HSK教程1·学练手册》中，供师生按需选用，以保证学习者在顺利通过一级考试的同时，更好地提升中文交际能力。

##### 课文板块

本书课文板块包括目标、课文、小语讲堂、综合练习、课堂活动、小语的彩蛋、学习小结等。第1~3课设置“跟读绕口令”板块，第4~15课增加“热身”板块。

目标：提示当课教学、学习重点。

热身：对课文及生词的预习、复习，贴近HSK3.0考试题型。

课文：每课包含三篇课文，均为情景对话。

小语讲堂：为语言点讲练。随文出现，便于学习者及时掌握。

综合练习：为三篇课文的整体练习。教师可灵活安排，或课上完成，或布置为课后作业。

课堂活动：设置双人活动、多人活动、角色扮演等，综合运用所学内容，完成交际任务。

小语的彩蛋：本书共设置八个。主要为与当课相关的必要的信息及文化点等，是课文内容的延伸与补充。

学习小结：学习后的总结与反思。每三课设置一个，供师生评估和反思教学、学习效果。

此外，本书还设置了“小语助力”，一般直接置于课文中相关内容附近，提示、注解重要信息，帮助学习者简单快速了解相关知识。

本书还配有学练手册及数字教材。数字教材可通过“外研国际智慧教育平台”（https://global.unipus.cn）获取。数字教材涵盖教材配套的音频、视频、交互式学习内容、补充练习等丰富资源。数字教材激活说明请扫描封底二维码查看。本书配套资源可通过“外研社汉语教学资源网”（http://www.fltrp-clt.com）查看并下载。

# Instructions for Use

New HSK Course 1 is designed for complete beginners in Chinese as well as learners preparing for the HSK (Level 1) exam.

##### Basic Design

Based on the new HSK syllabus and the design philosophy and principles of this textbook series, New HSK Course 1 consists of 15 lessons, includes vocabulary aligned with the 300 words required by the new syllabus with slight extensions, and covers 40 language points. The recommended total teaching time is 30–36 class hours, with 2–3 class hours suggested for each lesson.

New HSK Course 1 follows a learner-centered philosophy and adopts a goal-oriented, needs-based approach. Guided by the principle of cultivating learners' Chinese communicative competence, the course integrates systematic instruction in Pinyin and Chinese characters, presented in “Xiaoyu’s Bonus Content” in Lesson 1 and Lesson 7 respectively. These components are also available in New HSK Course 1 Workbook for flexible use by teachers and students, helping learners not only pass the HSK (Level 1) exam but also improve their practical Chinese communication skills.

##### Lesson Section

The lesson section of this book includes Objectives, Texts, Xiaoyu's Classroom, Comprehensive Exercises, Classroom Activity, Xiaoyu's Bonus Content and Learning Summary. Lessons 1–3 include a "Shadow the Tongue Twister" section, while Lessons 4–15 incorporate a "Warm-Up" section.

Objectives: Key points of teaching and learning in each lesson.

Warm-Up: Preview and review of lesson content and new vocabulary, closely aligned with HSK 3.0 exam question types.

Texts: Three situational dialogues in each lesson.

Xiaoyu's Classroom: Explanations and practice of language points, appearing after each text for timely learning.

Comprehensive Exercises: Integrated exercises covering all three texts, arranged flexibly for in-class or after-class use.

Classroom Activity: Pair work, group work, and role-play for communicative task completion using learned content.

Xiaoyu's Bonus Content: Eight such sections providing essential cultural and informational points related to each lesson.

Learning Summary: Review and reflection section appearing every three lessons for evaluation of teaching and learning outcomes.

In addition, this book features “Xiaoyu's Tip”, brief notes placed near relevant content to help learners quickly grasp related knowledge.

The book comes with a workbook and a digital edition, accessible via the FLTRP Global Smart Education Platform (https://global.unipus.cn). This platform offers audio, video, interactive modules, and extra exercises. To activate your digital edition, please scan the QR code located on the back cover. Additional teaching and learning resources can be viewed and downloaded on FLTRP CLT Resources (http://www.fltrp-clt.com).

# 内容梗概及主要人物

##### Content Summary and Main Characters

本册内容在海外和中国国内两大场景中展开。海外场景为中国外派国际中文教师王一飞和其学生以及在海外的中国留学生李文共同演绎的校园学习和生活故事；中国国内场景主要展示王一飞的堂姐王一雪一家的日常家庭生活，以及刘明和王一雪夫妇等人在医院、公司的职场生活。

The content of this volume unfolds in two main settings: overseas and in China. The overseas setting portrays the campus life and studies of Wang Yifei, an international Chinese language teacher from China, together with her students and Li Wen, a Chinese international student studying abroad. The domestic setting mainly presents the everyday family life of Wang Yixue, Wang Yifei's cousin, and her family, as well as the professional lives of Wang Yixue and Liu Ming, her husband, and others in workplaces such as the hospital and the company.

## 一、教师用语 Classroom Language for Teachers

Xiànzài shàngkè.

1. 现在上课。

Class begins now.

Qǐng dǎkāi shū, dì yè.

2. 请打开书，第×页。

Please open your books to page ×.

Jintian xuéxí dì kè.

3. 今天学习第 $ \times $课。

Today we're studying Lesson  $ \times $.

Zhè shì shénme?

4. 这是什么？

What's this?

Qịng gên wồ dú.

5. 请跟我读。

Please repeat after me.

Qĩng nĩ dú.

6. 请你读。

Please read aloud.

Qǐng zài dú yí biàn.

7. 请再读一遍。

Please read it aloud again.

Qìng nǐ wèn, tā / tā huí dá.

8. 请你问，他/她回答。

You ask the question, and he/she will answer.

Qing zuò.

9. 请坐。

Please sit down.

Xiàkè

10. 下课。

Class dismissed.

## 二、学生用语 Classroom Language for Students

Qịng zài shuō yí biàn.

1. 请再说一遍。

Could you repeat that, please?

Qăng zài dú yí biàn.

2. 请再读一遍。

Please read it aloud again.

Lăoshī, qǐng màn diǎnr shuō.

3. 老师，请慢点儿说。

Teacher, could you speak more slowly?

Zhège shì shénme yìsi?

4. 这个是什么意思?

What does this mean?

# AI小语，你好！

Hello, AI Xiaoyu!

#### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并使用礼貌用语打招呼、致谢、告别。Be able to understand and use polite expressions for greetings, thanks, and farewells.

了解中文交际礼仪，能听懂并使用第二人称代词敬称“您”。Understand Chinese communication etiquette, and be able to understand and use the honorific second-person pronoun“您”.

#### 课文1 Text1

开学第一天，在办公室里，王一飞和AI助教小语打招呼。On the first day of school, in the office, Wang Yifei was greeting the AI assistant Xiaoyu.

朗读对话。Read the dialogue aloud. ☑ 1-1

##### 生词 New Words (1-4)

大家 dàjiā

pron. everybody

adj. good; fine

used after a personal

pronoun or a noun to show plural number

⑥ 老师 lǎoshī n. teacher

⑧ 你们 nǐmen pron. you

pron. (plural) you

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

两人一组或多人一组，用真实姓名对话。Work in pairs or groups and have a dialogue using your real names.

#### 跟读绕口令 Shadow the Tongue Twister (1-7)

Mã zhòng má, wǒ fàng mǎ.

妈种麻，我放马。

Mǎ chī má, mā mà mǎ.

马吃麻，妈骂马。

Mom plants flax. I tend the horse.

The horse eats the flax. Mom scolds the horse.

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂并使用中文姓名自我介绍。Be able to understand others' self-introductions and introduce oneself using a Chinese name.

能听懂并使用致歉语，用“对不起”和“没关系”表达歉意和回应。Be able to understand and use expressions of apology, including saying “对不起” to apologize and responding with“没关系”.

掌握汉语的基本语序。Master the basic word order in Chinese.

□ 了解中文姓名的构成。Understand the structure of Chinese names.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 汉语的基本语序 Basic Word Order in Chinese

汉语的基本语序是：主语+谓语+宾语。例如：我（主语）叫（谓语）陈天中（宾语）。

The basic word order in Chinese is: Subject+Predicate+Object. For example: 我 (Subject) 叫 (Predicate) 陈天中 (Object)。

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）你叫什么名字？

（2）我叫白家月。

（3）我是学生。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 “是”字句“是”Sentence

表示人或事物等同什么或类属什么，否定形式是“不是”。

The “是” sentence is used to indicate what somebody or something equals or belongs to. The negative form is “不是”.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我是法国人。

（2）她是中文老师。

（3）我老师不是法国人。

### 2 结构助词“的” Structural Particle“的”

结构助词“的”在定语和中心语之间，表达领属关系。

The structural particle “的” is placed between the attributive and the head noun/nominal phrase to express a possessive relationship.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）白家月的中文老师

（2）你的名字

如果“的”前边是人称代词，后边是亲属称谓或指人的名词，“的”可省略。

If the word “的” is preceded by a personal pronoun and followed by a kinship term or a noun referring to a person, “的” can be omitted.

##### 大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我老师（2）我学生

（3）你同学 （4）我妈妈（māma, mother）

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：这是我（）。

B：老师，您好！

（2）李文：请问，你（）叫什么名字？

白家月：我（）叫王一飞。

“谁”也可以读成“shuí”。

Chen Tianzhong: She is also Thai.

Wang Yixue: Hi, Yifei!

Wang Yifei: Sister!

Wang Yixue: Are you still busy with work?

Wang Yifei: Yes, I'm still very busy. Are you busy too?

Wang Yixue: I'm not too busy. We miss you a lot.

Wang Yifei: I miss you all too.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 3 用“吗”的是非问句 Yes-No Question with“吗”

“吗”是语气助词，通常在句子末尾，表示疑问。基本结构：……吗？

The word “吗” is a modal particle typically placed at the end of a sentence to indicate a yes-no question with “吗”. Basic structure: ……吗?

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）你也很忙吗？

（2）你是他的中文老师吗？

（3）你有（yǒu, have）姐姐吗？

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂并使用“有”字句（1）表达领有人或物品。Be able to understand and use the “有” sentence (1) to express possession of people or objects.

□ 掌握“A……，B呢？”的用法。Master the use of“A……，B呢？”.

掌握本课数词、量词的用法。Master the use of the numerals and measure words introduced in this lesson.

了解中国人如何问答年龄，能使用合适的词语进行年龄问答。Understand how Chinese people ask and answer about age, and be able to use appropriate words for age-related questions and answers.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 “有”字句（1）“有”Sentence(1)

本课“有”表示领有，否定形式为“没/没有”。

The word “有” in this lesson indicates possession. Its negative form is “没/没有”.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）她有二十个学生。

（2）她有一个姐姐。

（3）我没/没有姐姐。

### 2 数字的表达 Expression of Numbers

汉语中，数字有阿拉伯数字和汉字两种写法，例如“1”和“—”。

In Chinese, numbers can be written in two forms: Arabic numerals (e.g., “1”) and Chinese characters (e.g., “—”).

（1）0~99的写法和读法 The writing and pronunciation of numbers 0-99

（3）“2”的写法和读法 The writing and pronunciation of “2”

数字 “2” 写作、读作 “二（èr）” 或 “两（liǎng）”。一般情况下，在序数词里用 “二（èr）”，如 “第二”；在量词前用 “两（liǎng）”。

The number "2" can be written and pronounced as either "二 (èr)" or "两 (liǎng)". Generally, "二 (èr)" is used in ordinal numbers, such as "第二", while "两 (liǎng)" is used before measure words.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

##### 生词 New Words ☐)4-4

④ 哥哥 gēge n. elder brother

⑤呢 ne part. used at the end of a question

⑥ 没有 méiyǒu v. not have

⑦ 家 jiā n. family

⑧ 几 jǐ pron. how many

⑨ 口 kǒu m. for family members, knives, etc.

⑩ 爸爸 bàba n. father

⑪ 妈妈 māma n. mother

⑫ 妹妹 mèimei n. younger sister

⑬ 和 hé conj. and

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

两人一组，根据实际情况对话。Work in pairs and have a dialogue based on real-life situations.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 3 语气助词“呢”（1）Modal Particle“呢”(1)

本课“呢”在句末，表示疑问，询问上文提到的情况。基本结构：A……，B呢？

The word “呢” in this lesson is used at the end of a sentence to indicate a question, inquiring about the situation mentioned previously. Basic structure: A……，B呢？

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我有两个哥哥，你呢？

（2）我叫白家月，你呢？

（3）我是中国人，你呢？

### 4 名量词和名量结构 Nominal Measure Words and [Num+M+(N)] Structure

表达数量时，汉语数词后一般要带量词。本课学习表示人或物数量的名量词，如“口、个”等，以及名量结构，如“四口人、两个哥哥”等。

When expressing quantity, a Chinese numeral is generally followed by a measure word. In this lesson, we focus on nominal measure words that indicate the quantity of people or objects, such as “口” and “个”, and the [Num+M+(N)] structure, such as “四口人” and “两个哥哥”.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）四口

（2）四口人

（3）两个

（4）两个学生

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

(1) A: 你家有( )?

B: 我家有三口人，你家呢?

A: 我家有( )。

（2）A：你有姐姐吗？

B：我有（）姐姐，你呢？

A：我有两个姐姐。

王一雪

Wáng Yīxuě

王一雪

Tā jīnnián shí'èr.

她今年十二。

Yang Tongle: Is this your son?

Wang Yixue: Yes. I have two children, a son and a daughter.

Yang Tongle: How old is your son?

Wang Yixue: He is five years old.

Yang Tongle: How old is your daughter?

Wang Yixue: She is twelve.

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

（1）王一雪有几个儿子？几个女儿？How many sons and daughters does Wang Yixue have?

（2）王一雪儿子今年几岁？

How old is Wang Yixue's son?

生词 New Words ☑)4-6

⑭ 儿子 érzi n. son

⑮ 孩子 háizi n. children

⑯ 女儿 nǚ'ér n. daughter

⑰ 岁 suì m. year (of age)

⑱ 他 tā pron. he; him

⑲ 今年 jīnnián n. this year

 $ ^{20} $多 duō pron. how; to what extent

②1 大 dà adj. (of age) old

# 今天我休息

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂、看懂并按照中文习惯描述事件、行为发生的日期。Be able to understand, read and describe the dates of events or actions according to Chinese conventions.

☐ 能听懂并使用能愿动词“会”表达懂得或有能力做事情。Be able to understand and use the modal verb“会”to express the knowledge or ability to perform an action.

掌握名词谓语句的用法。Master the use of nominal-predicate sentences.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 时间的表达（1） Expression of Time (1)

本课学习日期、星期的表达。汉语日期、星期的表达顺序是：年→月→日/号→星期。

This lesson focuses on how to express dates and days of the week in Chinese. The sequence for stating dates and days of the week is: 年→月→日/号→星期.

##### 大声朗读。Read aloud.

yī jiǔ jiǔ èr nián wǔ yuè shíliù rì / hào, Xīngqīliù
1992 年 5 月 16 日/号，星期六 Saturday, May 16, 1992
èr líng èr sì nián shí yuè èrshí'èr rì / hào, Xīngqièr
2024 年 10 月 22 日/号，星期二 Tuesday, October 22, 2024



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Yīyuè 一月 January</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Éryuè 二月 February</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sānyuè 三月 March</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Siyuè 四月 April</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Wǔyuè 五月 May</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Liùyuè 六月 June</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Qīyuè 七月 July</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Bāyuè 八月 August</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Jiǔyuè 九月 September</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Shíyuè 十月 October</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Shíyīyuè 十一月 November</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Shí&#x27;èryuè 十二月 December</td></tr><tr><td colspan="6"></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqīyī 星期一 Monday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqī&#x27;èr 星期二 Tuesday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqī&#x27;sān 星期三 Wednesday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqīsān 星期三 Thursday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqīsi 星期四 Thursday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqīwǔ 星期五 Friday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqīliù 星期六 Saturday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Xīngqīrì / Xīngqītiān 星期日 / 星期天 Sunday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr></table>

今天9月8号。Today is September 8.
今天是2025年5月22号。Today is May 22, 2025.
明天是2024年8月18号，星期天。Tomorrow is Sunday, August 18, 2024.

### 2 名词谓语句 Nominal-Predicate Sentences

名词谓语句谓语部分只有名词或名词性成分，这部分词语一般是表达时间、日期、年龄等的词语。

In a nominal-predicate sentence, the predicate consists only of a noun or a nominal phrase, usually used to indicate time, date, age, and so on.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）A：今天几号？ B：今天5月1号。

（2）我妹妹12岁。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

3 能愿动词“会” Modal Verb“会”

“会”用在动词前，表示通过学习后，懂得怎样做或有能力做。

The word “会” placed before a verb indicates the knowledge or ability to perform an action that is acquired through learning.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）你会做饭吗？（2）我会做面条儿。

（3）我不会做菜。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 小组游戏 Group Game

一个人拿着词语卡片，询问其他人会不会做这件事，其他人抢答。抢答最少者成为下一轮的提问者。

A student holds word cards and asks others whether they can do the actions on the cards. The others race to answer, and the one who gives the fewest answers becomes the next questioner.

# 你的手机号是多少？

# What's your cell phone number?

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂手机号码并使用号码拨打手机。Be able to understand cell phone numbers and use them to make calls.

能听懂并使用两个或多个动词性短语表达动作的目的或方式。Be able to understand and use two or more verbal phrases to express the purpose or manner of an action.

掌握能愿动词“想”的用法。Master the use of the modal verb“想”.

掌握疑问代词“怎么”询问方式的用法。Master the use of the interrogative pronoun “怎么” to ask about the manner or method of an action.

两人一组，根据实际情况对话。Work in pairs and have a dialogue based on real-life situations.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

1 能愿动词“想” Modal Verb“想”

能愿动词“想”用在动词前，表示希望、打算。

The modal verb “想”, when used before a verb, indicates desire or intention.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我想去超市。（2）我哥哥不想休息。

面条儿

##### 评价(3994)

三层楼有包间65 工作日不需排队9 面条儿推荐必点

一楼服务特别热情130 包子很好吃60 拌面面

这家饭店开了很多年了，菜做得地道有特色，好吃！

Wang Yixue: Where are we going for dinner on Sunday?

Liu Ming: I'd like to go to Xi'an Restaurant again.

Liu Xiaoxue: The steamed stuffed buns there are very delicious—I’d love to eat some.

Liu Xiaoming: Mom, I'd rather have rice—not steamed stuffed buns.

Wang Yixue: Alright. How shall we get there?

Liu Ming: Let's take a taxi.

（2）他们怎么去西安饭店？How will they go to Xi'an Restaurant?

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 2 连动句（1） Serial Verb Sentences (1)

连动句的谓语部分由两个或两个以上动词性短语构成。连动句有两种意义：

A serial verb sentence consists of two or more verbal phrases in the predicate. It typically expresses two types of meanings:

（1）表示动作的目的 Indicating the purpose of an action

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我想去超市买东西。

（2）我们去西安饭店吃晚饭。

##### （2）表示动作的方式 Indicating the manner of an action

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我们坐出租车去西安饭店。

（2）她坐出租车去超市。

### 3 疑问代词“怎么” Interrogative Pronoun“怎么”

疑问代词“怎么”用在动词前，询问方式。

The interrogative pronoun “怎么” is used before a verb to ask about the manner or method of an action.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我们怎么去？

（2）她怎么去超市？

# 我晚上六点半下班

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并描述事情发生的时间。Be able to understand and describe the timing of events.

☐ 掌握语气助词“吧”（1）表达建议、商量等的用法。Master the use of the modal particle “吧”(1) to express suggestions, consultations, and so on.

☐ 掌握语气助词“呢”（2）表达确认的事实的用法。Master the use of the modal particle“呢”(2) to express confirmed facts.

掌握副词、时间词语作状语的位置。Master the position of adverbs and time expressions as adverbials.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 时间的表达（2） Expression of Time (2)

##### （1）使用“点、分”表达时间点 Using “点” and “分” to express specific times

“点、分”表示具体时间点，整点使用“点”，不是整点使用“分”，“分”可以省略。但是，当时间为“十分”时，不能省略“分”；当时间为十分以下时，要读出“零”。

The words “点” and “分” are used to indicate specific points in time. “点” is used for whole hours, while “分” is used when minutes are involved. The word “分” can be omitted. However, when the time is exactly ten minutes, “分” cannot be omitted. For times less than ten minutes, “零” must be pronounced.

##### 大声朗读。Read aloud.



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>点</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>分</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>数字写法（numerical notation）</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>九点</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>9:00</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>二十点</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>20:00</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>十五点</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>四十</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>15:40</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>二十一点</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>十分</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>21:10</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>二十二点</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>零五</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>22:05</td></tr></table>

（2）使用 “上午、中午、下午、晚上” 等时间名词表达时间段 Using time nouns such as “上午”，“中午”，“下午”，and “晚上” to express time periods

“上午、中午、下午、晚上”等时间名词可以表达时间段。这些时间名词后还可以直接加时间点。

Time nouns such as “上午”, “中午”, “下午”, and “晚上” can be used to express time periods, and they can be directly followed by a specific time.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）上午 （2）中午十二点

（3）下午两点半 （4）晚上九点十分

### 2 语气助词“吧”（1）Modal Particle“吧”（1）

本课语气助词“吧”读轻声（ba），在句子末尾，表示建议、商量、劝告、请求。

The modal particle “吧” in this lesson is pronounced with a neutral tone (ba) at the end of a sentence. It is used to express suggestions, consultations, advice, or requests.

朗读对话。Read the dialogues aloud.

（1）A：我们下午三点见吧。

B: 好的。

(2) A: 你去超市买吧。

B: 超市没有。

（3）A：我们去西安饭店吃晚饭吧。

B: 好的。

两人一组，根据实际情况对话。Work in pairs and have a dialogue based on real-life situations.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

3 副词、时间词语作状语的位置 Position of Adverbs and Time Expressions as Adverbials 在汉语中，副词、时间词语作状语时，一般都要在动词或形容词前。

In Chinese, when adverbs and time expressions function as adverbials, they are generally placed before verbs or adjectives.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我不想去。

（2）妹妹很高兴。

（3）她上午十点半上课。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：我们（）点见？ B：我们下午（）见吧。

（2）A：你上午去上课，是吗？ B：对，我（）有课。

（3）A：我们（）去超市吧。 B：对不起，我（）有课。

### 4 语气助词“呢”（2）Modal Particle“呢”(2)

本课语气助词“呢”位于句子末尾，表示确认的事实。基本结构：……呢。

The modal particle “呢” in this lesson is placed at the end of a sentence to indicate confirmed facts. Basic structure: ……呢。

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我明天下午两点还上课呢。

（2）妹妹会做两个菜呢。

（3）李文晚上还有事呢。

#### 课文3 Text 3

王一雪在办公室给刘明打电话。Wang Yixue was calling Liu Ming at the office.

听两遍对话，选择正确答案。Listen to the dialogue twice and choose the correct answer. 7-5

Liu Ming ne.

Wang Yixue: Hey, where are you?

Liu Ming: I'm at home.

Wang Yixue: I'll finish work at 6:30 in the evening.

Liu Ming: I'll go to work at the hospital at 8:00.

Wang Yixue: Alright, could you go to the store to buy some vegetables?

Liu Ming: Okay, I'll go in ten minutes.

“里”作方位词时，在“家、超市”等名词性词语后，一般读轻声“li”；在“里边”等词语中，需读第三声

“li”。When “里” is used as a positional word, it is generally pronounced in the neutral tone after nouns/nominal phrases like “家” and “超市”，but should be pronounced in the third tone in words such as “里边”.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//f407348c-3bca-4978-9d48-00c49bce75db/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_623_427_1076_749.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T10%3A03%3A41Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe44655a585a722e93c1546268b9e8266dd3ebf1f06c0c857d71328ca16cca15d" alt="Image" width="38%" /></div>


##### 生词 New Words ☐)7-6

⑱ 在 zài v. be at/in/on

⑲ 家 jiā n. home

②0里li/lǐ n. inner; inside

②1 晚上 wǎnshang n. evening

② 医院 yīyuàn n. hospital

②3 上班 shàngbān v. go to work

 $ ^{24} $店 diàn n. shop; store

②6分钟 fēnzhōng m. minute

 $ ^{27} $后 hòu n. after; later

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

根据课文内容回答问题。Answer the questions according to the text.

（1）刘明晚上还去做什么？What will Liu Ming do in the evening?

（2）刘明十分钟后去做什么？What will Liu Ming do in ten minutes?

A 医院 B 十二点半 C 课 D 现在 E 见

（1）她星期天没有 ___。

（2）我们中午_____下课，下午两点_____吧。

（3）白家月：对不起，我_____有事呢，你去吧。

李文：好的，你忙吧。

（4）王一雪：你今天晚上几点去_____上班？

刘明：晚上八点。

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并描述处所、位置等信息。Be able to understand and describe locations, positions, and related details.

☐ 掌握介词“在”表示方位的用法。Master the use of the preposition“在”to indicate direction/location.

掌握能愿动词“能”的用法。Master the use of the modal verb“能”.

量词可以和指示代词直接组合。Measure words can directly combine with demonstrative pronouns.

Bai Jiayue: There is a little cat outside the room.

Chen Tianzhong: I don't see it. Where is it?

Bai Jiayue: It's under the table.

Chen Tianzhong: This little cat is so beautiful!

##### 生词 New Words (1) 8-2

① 房间 fángjiān n. room

② 外 wài n. outside

③ 只 zhī m. for certain animals

④ 小 xiǎo adj. small; little

⑤ 猫 māo n. cat

⑥ 没 méi adv. no; not; not yet

看见 kànjiàn v. see; catch sight of

⑧ 桌子 zhuōzi n. table; desk

⑨ 下 xià n. low position or rank

⑩ 漂亮 piàoliang adj. pretty; beautiful

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 方位词 Positional Words

本册学习的表达方向、位置的方位词有：上、下、里、外、前、后、外边。

The positional words expressing direction and location introduced in this volume are: 上, 下, 里, 外, 前, 后, and 外边.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）房间里有一只小猫。

（2）我们去书店外吧。

（3）小雪的手机在桌子上呢。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 2 介词“在” Preposition“在”

介词“在”和表示位置、处所的词语组合，在动词前，表示在什么位置、处所做什么。

The preposition “在” combines with words or phrases that indicate a location or place. This structure is placed before the verb to show where an action takes place.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我在学校吃午饭。

（2）他爸爸在医院工作。

（3）你在哪儿买菜？

### 3 能愿动词“能” Modal Verb“能”

能愿动词“能”位于动词前，表示有能力、有条件或可能做某事。

The modal verb “能” is placed before the verb to indicate the ability, condition, or possibility of doing something.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）下午两点你能到吗？

（2）爸爸能去。

（3）我不能去学校吃午饭。

②医生

##### 专有名词 Proper Noun

① 胡医生 Hú yīshēng Dr. Hu

Liu Ming: Xiao Hu, haven't you eaten yet?

Dr. Hu: No, I haven't.

Liu Ming: There are a lot of patients in big hospitals, and doctors are very busy.

Dr. Hu: True. My father also works at a hospital, and he's extremely busy too.

Liu Ming: So there are two doctors in your family?

Dr. Hu: Yes.

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

根据课文内容回答问题。Answer the questions according to the text.

（1）胡医生的爸爸在哪儿工作？Where does Dr. Hu's father work?

（2）胡医生的爸爸工作忙吗？Is Dr. Hu's father busy with his job?

#### 综合练习 Comprehensive Exercises

选词填空。Choose the proper words to fill in the blanks.

A 哪儿 B 在 C 桌子 D 书店 E 能

（1）胡医生的爸爸___医院工作。

（2）我下午两点 $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $到学校。

（3）陈天中：小猫在_____呢？

白家月：小猫在_____下。

# 我明天上午在学校学习

# I'll be studying at school tomorrow morning

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂并使用“有/是”表达存在。Be able to understand and use“有/是”to indicate existence.

掌握时间词语和处所词语同现时的顺序。Master the sequence of time and location expressions when they co-occur.

□ 掌握“第”的用法。Master the use of“第”.

□ 了解当代中国人的休闲活动。Understand the leisure activities of contemporary Chinese people.

“前边”可儿化，说成“前边儿”。“边”是后缀，读轻声。“边”做名词时，读成“biān”。“前边”can be pronounced with a retroflex suffix as“前边儿”。Here，“边”functions as a suffix and is pronounced with a neutral tone. When“边”functions as a noun, it is pronounced as“biān”.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 存现句（1） Existential Sentences (1)

存现句是表示某个地方或位置存在某人或某物的句子，一般以“有”或“是”为标记。否定时，宾语前不能使用数量词语。

Existential sentences indicate the existence of someone or something in a specific place or location, generally marked by “有” or “是”. When negating, no numeral-measure word phrase is used before the object.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）学校前边有一家电影院。（2）电影院前边是一家超市。

（3）电影院前边不是超市。（4）桌子上没有小猫。

### 2 时间词语和处所词语同时作状语的顺序 Sequence of Time and Location Expressions When Used as Adverbials Simultaneously

时间词语和处所词语同时作状语时，时间词语要在处所词语前。

When time and location expressions function as adverbials simultaneously, the time expressions should precede the location expressions.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我们七点在电影院外边见。（2）安妮下午在家里学中文。

（3）陈天中明天中午在学校吃午饭。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你们今天下午两点（）上课？ B：在学校。

（2）A：你们（）在超市买什么呢？ B：买菜呢。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8ff03a93-3f6e-46dc-8b5a-6befc8f472d7/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_116_260_215_469.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T10%3A03%3A46Z%2F-1%2F%2F73911966097d0b47ea8c719a97103cdac30634b00539d62345e4fc32a07081b3" alt="Image" width="8%" /></div>


作方位词时，“上”在名词后，比如“椅子上”“书上”，读轻声；表示位置在高处，或次序、时间在前时，比如“上方”“上半年”，“上”读四声。When used as a positional word，“上”is pronounced with a neutral tone when it follows a noun，as in“椅子上”and“书上”。However，when indicating a higher position，or precedence in sequence or time，as in“上方”and“上半年”，it is pronounced with the fourth tone.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8ff03a93-3f6e-46dc-8b5a-6befc8f472d7/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_123_537_199_658.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T10%3A03%3A47Z%2F-1%2F%2F7f8cf617f431fb5be149c8a30985c3c21f7059d0f72b38bf4f15ebc72777d3a7" alt="Image" width="6%" /></div>

##### 生词 New Words ☐)9-4

⑥ 椅子 yǐzi n. chair

⑦ 上 shang/n. noun, indicating position/higher position

⑧ 本 běn m. for books

⑨ 书 shū n. book

⑩ 那 nà pron. that

⑪ 第 di pref. marker of ordinal numbers

 $ ⑫ $ 学习 xuéjí v. study; learn

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

根据课文内容回答问题。Answer the questions according to the text.

（1）椅子上的书是谁的？Whose book is on the chair?

（2）陈天中明天上午在哪儿学习？Where will Chen Tianzhong be studying tomorrow morning?

Bai Jiayue: There is a Chinese book on the chair. Whose book is that?

Chen Tianzhong: It's my book. Thank you. This is my second Chinese book.

Bai Jiayue: You're welcome. Where will you be tomorrow morning?

Chen Tianzhong: I'll be studying at school tomorrow morning.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 3 表示序数的“第”“第”Indicating Ordinal Numbers

“第”位于整数的数词前，表示序数。基本结构：（1）第+数词；（2）第+数词+量词+（名词）。

“第” precedes cardinal numbers to indicate ordinal numbers. Basic structures: (1) 第+ Numeral; (2) 第+ Numeral+ Measure Word+(Noun).

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）第一，第二，第三 （2）第一个，第二个，第一本

（3）第一个学生，第一本书

play; have fun

Wang Yixue: Tomorrow is Saturday.

What are you going to do?

Yang Tongle: I will read at home during the day and go out to sing with my friends in the evening.

Wang Yixue: You sing very well.

Yang Tongle: Thank you! What will you do on Saturday?

Wang Yixue: I will cook, watch TV, and play with my children and my little dog at home.

Yang Tongle: I also have a little dog.

我需要努力的 Points I Need to Improve

# 这儿的苹果真便宜！

# The apples here are really affordable!

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂、看懂并简单谈论商品价格。Be able to comprehend and recognize product prices, and talk about them simply.

☐ 掌握形容词谓语句的用法。Master the use of adjectival-predicate sentences.

□ 掌握疑问代词“怎么样”的用法。Master the use of the interrogative pronoun“怎么样”.

□ 认识人民币。Recognize Renminbi (RMB).

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 钱数的表达 Expression of Amount of Money

中国的货币叫人民币。人民币的单位由大到小是“元、角、分”，口语中也分别说“块、毛、分”。表达顺序是：元/块→角/毛→分。

China’s currency is known as Renminbi (RMB). Its units, ranked from the largest to the smallest, are “元”, “角”, and “分”, which are colloquially referred to as “块”, “毛”, and “分” respectively. The standard order of denomination is: 元/块→角/毛→分.

### 2 形容词谓语句 Adjectival-Predicate Sentences

形容词可以直接作谓语，前面可用程度副词或否定副词。

Adjectives can be used directly as predicates, with adverbs of degree or negative adverbs optionally placed before them.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）这儿的水果真不少！（2）我的房间不大。

（3）那个苹果好吃。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 3 疑问代词“怎么样” Interrogative Pronoun“怎么样”

疑问代词“怎么样”用于征求意见、询问状况等。基本结构：……怎么样？

The interrogative pronoun “怎么样” is used to ask for opinions or inquire about a situation, and so on. Basic structure: ……怎么样?

朗读对话。Read the dialogues aloud.

（1）A：这个杯子怎么样？

B：我很喜欢，也不贵。

（2）A：这本书怎么样？

B：很好看。

（3）A：这个菜怎么样？

B：这个菜不太好吃，我不喜欢。

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂并使用三种结构表达正在做的事情。Be able to understand and use three structures to express actions in progress.

□ 能听懂并使用正反问格式进行提问。Be able to understand and form questions using the affirmative-negative question pattern.

掌握能愿动词“要”表达想做、打算做的用法。Master the use of the modal verb “要” to express the desire or intention to do something.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 正反问 Affirmative-Negative Questions

正反问格式是“×+不/没+×”，“×”是动词或形容词。动词正反问使用“不/没”，形容词正反问使用“不”。

Affirmative-negative questions follow the pattern “×+不/没+×”，where “×” is a verb or an adjective. “不/没” is used in verb affirmative-negative questions, while “不” is used in adjective affirmative-negative questions.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）它是不是在超市后边？（2）你去没去学校？

（3）这件衣服好看不好看？

朗读对话。Read the dialogues aloud.

（1）A：他去哪儿了？你知道不知道？

B：我不知道。

（2）A：昨天你去没去书店？

B：我没去。

（3）A：这件衣服贵不贵？

B：不贵。

Wang Yifei: Are you still studying at university?

Li Wen: Yes. I'm studying at university, and I'm still an undergraduate.

Wang Yifei: Are you busy with your studies?

Li Wen: Very busy. I major in medicine, and we have a lot of classes.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 2 时间副词“在/正在” Temporal Adverbs“在/正在”

时间副词 “在/正在” 位于动词前，表示动作正在进行或情况在继续。本课有两种形式：（1）在/正在+动词；（2）在/正在+动词+呢。此外，表达正在做的事情还有第三种形式：（3）动词+呢。否定回答时，使用副词 “没（有）”。

The temporal adverbs “在/正在” are placed before a verb, expressing that an action is ongoing or a situation is continuing. This lesson introduces two forms: (1) 在/正在+Verb; (2) 在/正在+Verb+呢. Additionally, there is a third form to express an ongoing action: (3) Verb+呢. For negative responses, the adverb “没(有)” is used.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）你还在读大学吗？

（2）学生们在/正在上课呢。

（3）我们读书呢。

朗读对话。Read the dialogues aloud.

（1）A：你在做什么呢？ B：我正在看电视。

（2）A：我去看电影，你去不去？ B：我在学习呢，不想去。

（3）A：你在买菜吗？ B：我没买菜，买水果呢。

星期六早上，刘明要去医院加班，出门前和女儿对话。On Saturday morning, Liu Ming was going to the hospital to work overtime. Before leaving, he talked with his daughter.

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

根据课文内容回答问题。Answer the questions according to the text.

（1）弟弟起床没起床？Has the younger brother gotten up yet?

（2）弟弟今天要做什么？What is the younger brother going to do today?

Liu Ming: Has your younger brother gotten up yet?

Liu Xiaoxue: No, he hasn't gotten up yet. He's still sleeping.

Liu Ming: Still sleeping? Is he going there today?

Liu Xiaoxue: Where?

Liu Ming: To the supermarket.

Liu Xiaoxue: I asked him yesterday. He told me he wasn't going. He's going to play with his friends today.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

3 能愿动词“要” Modal Verb“要”

能愿动词“要”在动词前，表示想做、打算做。

When used before a verb, the modal verb “要” indicates the desire or intention to do something.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）他今天要和小朋友玩。

（2）妈妈要去超市。

（3）白家月要在家里学中文。

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并描述天气情况。Be able to understand and describe weather conditions.

☐ 能听懂并简单描述病情。Be able to understand and briefly describe the medical condition.

☐ 掌握语气助词 “了（1）” 的用法。Master the use of the modal particle “了(1)”.

☐ 掌握“太……了”格式的用法。Master the use of the“太……了”pattern.

了解生病时中国人建议“喝些热水”的好处。Understand the benefits of "drinking some warm water", as advised by Chinese people during illness.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 非主谓句 Non-Subject-Predicate Sentences

非主谓句是由词或短语构成、不分主语和谓语的句子，口语中常用。

Non-subject-predicate sentences are composed of words or phrases without distinct subjects or predicates, and are commonly used in colloquial speech.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）下雨了。 （2）下雪了。

（3）上课了。 （4）真漂亮！

（5）对不起！ （6）没关系！

### 2 语气助词 “了（1）” Modal Particle “了(1)”

语气助词 “了（1）” 位于句子末尾或句中停顿的地方，表示有变化或出现新情况。否定回答时，用副词 “没”，句子末尾不用 “了”。

The modal particle “ $ \neg $ $ 了 $ $  (1) $” is placed at the end of a sentence or at a pause within a sentence to indicate a change or a new situation. For negative responses, the adverb “ $ \neg $ $ 没 $ $ ” $ is used, and “ $ \neg $ $ 了 $ $ ” $ is omitted at the end of the sentence.

##### 大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）下雨了。 （2）十二点了，吃午饭吧。

（3）A：弟弟起床了吗？ B：没起床呢。

##### 生词 New Words ☐) 12-4

⑩ 下 xià v. (of rain, snow, etc.) come down; fall

⑪ 雪 xuě n. snow

⑫ 来 lái v. come

⑬ 公司 gōngsī n. company

⑭ 生病 shēngbìng v. fall ill

⑮ 看病 kànbìng v. (of a patient) see a doctor

Wang Yixue: It snowed yesterday.

Yang Tongle: Yes, it was so cold.

Wang Yixue: You didn't come to the company yesterday. Were you feeling unwell?

Yang Tongle: Yes, I went to the hospital to see a doctor.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

3 “太……了”格式“太……了”Pattern

“太……了”用于感叹程度很高或很深。

The “太……了” pattern expresses a very high or intense degree of exclamation.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）太冷了！

（2）这个杯子太小了。

（3）我们今天太高兴了！

Yang Tongle: Doctor, I'm not feeling well.

Doctor Hu: Let me take a look. How are you feeling?

Yang Tongle: I feel very cold.

Doctor Hu: Alright. Take some medicine and rest for half a day today.

Yang Tongle: OK.

Doctor Hu: Make sure to drink some warm water after you get home.

“看看”是“看”的重叠式，表示动作时间短。“看看”is the reduplicated form of“看”，indicating a brief action.

中国人喜欢喝热水，认为喝热水更健康。Chinese people like drinking warm water and believe that it is healthier.

形容词谓语句，例如：这个苹果很好吃。
Adjectival-predicate sentences, for example: 这个苹果很好吃。
疑问代词“怎么样”，例如：这件衣服怎么样？
The interrogative pronoun“怎么样”，for example: 这件衣服怎么样？
正反问，例如：好不好？
Affirmative-negative questions, for example: 好不好？
时间副词“在/正在”，例如：他在休息呢。
Temporal adverbs“在/正在”，for example: 他在休息呢。
能愿动词“要”，例如：我要睡觉。
The modal verb“要”，for example: 我要睡觉。
非主谓句，例如：下雨了。
Non-subject-predicate sentences, for example: 下雨了。
语气助词“了（1）”，例如：我去上课了。
The modal particle“了（1）”，for example: 我去上课了。
“太……了”格式，例如：太冷了！
The“太……了”pattern, for example: 太冷了！
描述天气情况，例如：今天下雨了。
Describe weather conditions, for example: 今天下雨了。
简单描述病情，例如：我病了，觉得很冷。
Briefly describe the medical condition, for example: 我病了，觉得很冷。

#### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并使用双宾语句（1）表示向别人提问或给予别人东西等。Be able to understand and use double-object sentences (1) to express asking someone questions or giving someone things, and so on.

掌握能愿动词“可以”的用法。Master the use of the modal verb“可以”.

□ 掌握“动词+一下”结构的用法。Master the use of the "Verb+一下" structure.

□ 了解点餐常用语。Understand common expressions for ordering food.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 能愿动词“可以” Modal Verb“可以”

能愿动词“可以”位于动词前，表示可能、能够或许可。

The modal verb “可以” is placed before a verb to indicate possibility, capability, or permission.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我可以再问您一个问题吗？

（2）你们可以看这本书。

（3）A：我可以坐吗？

B：可以，请坐！

### 2 “动词+一下”结构“Verb+一下”Structure

本课“动词+一下”结构表示做一次或试着做，动作时间短。

In this lesson, the “Verb+一下” structure indicates that an action is performed as a quick attempt or it is brief.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（2）请休息一下。

（1）你可以打电话问一下。

（3）你看一下吧。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 3 双宾语句（1） Double-Object Sentences (1)

双宾语句是一个动词带两个宾语的句子。本册学习“给、问”构成的双宾语句。

A double-object sentence is one where a verb takes two objects. In this volume, we focus on double-object sentences formed by “给” and “问”.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）请给我一杯牛奶。（2）白家月给安妮一个苹果。

（3）我问老师两个问题。

##### 生词 New Words ☑) 13-6

⑱ 先生 xiānsheng n. gentleman; sir

⑲ 一半 yíbàn num. one half; half

⑳ 茶 chá n. tea

Waiter: Sir, have a seat, please! What would you like to order?

Liu Ming: I'd like one jin of jiaozi.

Waiter: Alright. One jin of jiaozi has forty pieces.

Liu Ming: Forty is too many. I'll have half of that.

Waiter: Half is twenty. What would you like to drink?

Liu Ming: I'll have a cup of tea, please.

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并使用动态助词 “了（2）” 表示动作行为已经发生或完成。Be able to understand and use the aspect particle “了 (2)” to express the occurrence or completion of an action.

□ 掌握范围副词“都”的用法。Master the use of the scope adverb“都”.

掌握部分离合词的基本用法。Master the basic use of some separable words.

□ 了解并能描述学习情况。Understand and be able to describe learning process.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 1 动态助词“了（2）” Aspect Particle“了(2)”

动态助词 “了（2）” 在动词后，表示动作行为已经发生或完成。否定时用 “没”， 不加 “了”。

The aspect particle “了 (2)” is placed after a verb to indicate that an action has occurred or been completed. When negating, “没” is used instead, and “了” is omitted.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我看了一个电影。

（2）我买了一个新电脑。

（3）我昨天没去商店买东西。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你去哪儿（ ？

B：我今天有课，去学校上( )课，还在那儿吃( )午饭。

（2）A：我昨天看（）电影，（）去超市。你呢？

B：我昨天去（）超市，（）看电影。

### 2 离合词（1） Separable Words (1)

本册中的“上课、下课、上班、下班、说话、读书、睡觉、看病、生病”等是一类特殊的动词结构，可分可合，合在一起时是词，中间加其他成分则变成短语，所以被称为离合词。离合词分离时，可插入的成分有限。

In this volume, words such as “上课”，“下课”，“上班”，“下班”，“说话”，“读书”，“睡觉”，“看病”，and “生病” belong to a special type of verbal structure. These words can be either combined or separated: When combined, they function as single words; when elements are inserted between their components, they become phrases. Therefore, they are referred to as separable words. When separable words are split, the types of elements that can be inserted are limited.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

### 3 范围副词“都” Scope Adverb“都”

范围副词“都”表示全部、总括，总括的对象在“都”前。否定时，否定词在“都”后。

The scope adverb “都” means “all” or “in general”. The object being generalized is placed before “都”. When negating, the negative word is placed after “都”.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我们都会写了。

（2）我和我的朋友们都去。

（3）同学们都没听见。

Wang Yixue 王一雪

Shì de. Tài wǎn le, shuì jiào ba.

Liu Ming: Our daughter will start

middle school next year.

Wang Yixue: That's right. Our son will also start primary school.

Liu Ming: We will have a middle school student in our family.

Wang Yixue: And a primary school student.

Liu Ming: Once they start school, they will both be busy.

Wang Yixue: Yes. It's too late. Let's go to bed.

还有两个近似的代词，一个是“她们”，指称自己和对方以外的若干女性；另一个是“它们”，指称不止一个的事物等。三个词读音一样，但所指意义不同。There are two other similar pronouns: “她们”，referring to a group of females excluding the speaker and the listener, and “它们”，referring to more than one thing or object. These three words share the same pronunciation but differ in meaning.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//e17a762a-b595-4df8-a1d8-163f91b8ccc6/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_695_576_778_688.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T10%3A01%3A55Z%2F-1%2F%2F1f8238aadf626cb00ed015625a4fff2a7b5f9dfd4c885f1fabf9c5a3c41f42db" alt="Image" width="7%" /></div>


##### 生词 New Words ☑)14-6

⑯ 明年 míngnián n. next year

⑰ 上 shàng v. begin to do sth at a fixed time

⑱ 中学 zhōngxué n. middle school

⑲ 小学 xiǎoxué n.

②0 中学生 zhōngxuéshēng n.

②1 小学生 xiǎoxuéshēng n.

②② 上学 shàngxué v.

23 他们 tāmen pron. they; them

start school; go to school

②4 她们 tāmen pron. (referring to female people) they; them

它们 tāmen pron. (plural of 它) they; them; their

⑳ 晚 wǎn adj. late

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

（1）明年女儿和儿子都上小学吗？Will both the daughter and the son start primary school next year?

（2）上学后，他们忙不忙？Will they be busy after starting school?

我星期六上午上了中文课，中午吃了饭后休息了一下，下午去商店买了一些东西，晚上和朋友吃了晚饭。……

#### 目标 Objectives

□ 能听懂并使用相关词语表达旅行意愿、计划。Be able to understand and use relevant vocabulary to express travel intentions and plans.

☐ 掌握并列复句 “……，还/也……” 。Master the coordinate compound sentence “……，还/也……”

□ 了解中国餐桌文化、待客礼仪。Understand Chinese dining culture and hospitality etiquette.

□ 了解中国的首都——北京。Understand Beijing, the capital of China.

劝饭是一种中式餐桌文化，体现了中国人的热情好客。

Encouraging others to eat more is a part of Chinese dining culture that reflects the hospitality of Chinese people.

分角色朗读对话。Role-play the dialogue.

根据课文内容回答问题。Answer the questions according to the text.

（1）白家月爱吃哪个菜？Which dish does Bai Jiayue like?

（2）李文做饭好吃不好吃？Is Li Wen's cooking delicious or not?

Li Wen: Which dish do you like?

Bai Jiayue: I like this one, and I also like that one.

Chen Tianzhong: All of these dishes are delicious, and they look great too.

Li Wen: I like eating Chinese food, and I also like cooking it. Everyone, please help yourselves and eat more.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

1 并列复句 “……，还/也……” Coordinate Compound Sentence “……，还/也……”

并列复句由两个或两个以上逻辑关联、结构对称的小句构成。本册学习并列复句“……，还/也……”。

A coordinate compound sentence consists of two or more clauses that are logically related and structurally parallel. In this volume, we focus on the coordinate compound sentence “……，还/也……”.

大声朗读。Read aloud.

（1）我喜欢这个，也喜欢那个。

（2）王老师是北京人，李文也是北京人。

（3）我喜欢喝中国茶，还喜欢吃中国菜。

##### 生词 New Words ☐)15-4

③ 去年 qùn $ \underset{\cdot}{i} $án n. last year

④ 男朋友 nánpéngyou n. boyfriend

⑤ 几 jǐ num. a few; several

⑥ 年 nián n. year

⑦ 好玩儿 hǎowánr adj. fun; interesting

##### 专有名词 Proper Nouns

① 西安 Xī'ān Xi'an

② 北京 Běijīng Beijing

Annie: My boyfriend and I went to Xi'an last year, and I want to visit Beijing this year. Bai Jiayue: I went to Xi'an a few years ago, and it was really fun. This year, I want to go to Beijing too.

Li Wen: Where do you all want to go?

Li Wen: Ms. Wang and I are both from Beijing. Beijing is very beautiful.

Wang Yifei: How many hours will your flight take to get to Beijing?

Bai Jiayue: Nine hours.

Wang Yifei: My whole family lives in Beijing. My sister will be free on Sunday. She can pick you up at the airport, and you can stay at my home.

Annie: We’ll arrive at Daxing Airport at 8:00 on Sunday morning. Is that early?

Wang Yifei: Not early at all.

Bai Jiayue: Thank you, teacher! Then we'll meet your sister at Daxing Airport!

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

三个人分别扮演李文、陈天中和白家月，李文邀请陈天中和白家月做客，一起做中国菜。

Three students play the roles of Li Wen, Chen Tianzhong, and Bai Jiayue respectively. Li Wen invites Chen Tianzhong and Bai Jiayue to his home and cook Chinese food together.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5918c78f-9a6f-4794-aec9-e1ea85f79682/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_259_449_369_565.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-06T10%3A01%3A54Z%2F-1%2F%2F888e0b25ef4b06b3d57af2f3644e64e54f41fc78265f14e96efbef94f2ca30ae" alt="Image" width="9%" /></div>


李文：天中、家月，你们星期六有时间吗？

陈天中：我有时间。

白家月：我也有时间。

李文：太好了！你们都来我家吧，我们做中国菜吃，怎么样？……
